#### 配置容器

##### 1. EthSwtConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `cac441c7-5a9b-470d-83c4-bf69ebe0e3f6`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Configuration of one Ethernet Switch.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **18** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtIdx

- **UUID**: `08f103ef-405e-49b2-af68-1b097b50aa03`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtIdx`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the index used to address the switch within the BSW.

######## 2. EthSwtDefaultVlanId

- **UUID**: `10ed0bb8-a535-4791-9f1f-1cfc939f6fac`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtDefaultVlanId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the default VLAN ID used by the switch to handle un-tagged traffic internally on ports where no ingress VLAN tagging is enabled.

######## 3. EthSwtDefaultPcp

- **UUID**: `e61ecd7d-6f32-4d79-981e-792fa195df4f`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtDefaultPcp`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the default PCP used by the switch to handle un-tagged traffic internally on ports where no ingress VLAN tagging is enabled.

######## 4. EthSwtStaticVlanNum

- **UUID**: `910a99d2-7697-4485-a3bc-6b6b1080d212`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtStaticVlanNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4096]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of VLANs configured due to configuration settings applied to the switch ports.

######## 5. EthSwtOverallVlanNum

- **UUID**: `a46009b2-f2cd-436f-8895-46c3bb90156e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtOverallVlanNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4096]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of VLANs that can be handled during runtime.

This parameter represents the sum of static and dynamic VLANs.

######## 6. EthSwtDynamicVlanNum

- **UUID**: `79d55fae-425b-4ebf-95a5-22def5843dae`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtDynamicVlanNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4096]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of VLANs which are not related to any configuration settings that can be additionally handled during runtime.

######## 7. EthSwtMgmtFrameQueue

- **UUID**: `cd4fc30a-8da2-4818-ab5a-ddb5c484ef9d`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtMgmtFrameQueue`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `7`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the Ingress and Egress queue which is used for managed frames. If a shaper is configured for this queue, the manged frames will also be taken into account.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtEnableAddressAging

- **UUID**: `7c74ae4d-bd37-4fe3-a1c3-fc3e87091eb9`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtEnableAddressAging`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if address aging is applied to entries of the address table learned during runtime.

FALSE:
Address aging is not applied. Learned entries will not be forgotten.

TRUE:
Address aging is applied. Learned entries will be forgotten if no frame with a matching source MAC address is received for the timeout configured by EthSwtAddressAgeingTimeout.

######## 2. EthSwtGlobalTimeSupport

- **UUID**: `67e0f75a-e2cb-42b1-b03d-6f4a1281466f`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtGlobalTimeSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if port specific time stamping is enabled/disabled.

######## 3. EthSwtManagementSupport

- **UUID**: `8a2b2ea3-4c6e-4c68-9c51-7d10f06ad40e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtManagementSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The parameter defines if the management capabilities of the switch are enabled/disabled.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtAddressAgingTimeout

- **UUID**: `0970ac9b-a87b-4cf8-86dc-5caee2a92eed`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtAddressAgingTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `60000`
- **取值范围**: [10..327670]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the timeout after an entry of the address lookup table is forgotten if no frame with a matching source MAC address is received.

######## 2. EthSwtMgmtInfoIndicationTimeout

- **UUID**: `974f7588-3787-4aaf-b203-683b22059131`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtMgmtInfoIndicationTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0.1`
- **取值范围**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter isn't applicable for this driver.

######## 3. EthSwtTimeStampInfoIndicationTimeout

- **UUID**: `da66e2c9-126c-4806-8ff4-da7723ae08a2`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtTimeStampInfoIndicationTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0.001`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the timeout for fetching timestamps related to PTP frames.

If the timeout is reached the missing timestamp will be reported to the upper layer modules of the stack so they are able to take further actions based on their protocol specification.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtManagementDestMacs

- **UUID**: `49e59fba-d8b2-40f7-83c1-e9acd5b92dd7`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtManagementDestMacs`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `01:80:C2:00:00:0E`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the destination MAC address that is used for frames that must be managed.

######## 2. EthSwtManagementMetaFrameSrcMac

- **UUID**: `17ef5f00-de57-48e5-b2a6-5d96995eda94`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtManagementMetaFrameSrcMac`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `01:70:C2:AA:BB:CC`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the source MAC address of the META frames, which are generated by the Sja1105PQRS when FrameManagement / Global Time Support is enabled. The configured MAC address must be unique and and not used be other frames in the system.

######## 3. EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMarker

- **UUID**: `a3f6c844-2b9e-455e-8b59-2d3d8f9a95c6`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMarker`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `00:00:00:00`
  - 最大长度: 11
  - 最小长度: 11
- **描述**: This parameter and the parameter EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMask are used to decide if an Ethernet frame might be used for a stream or not.

The four most significant bytes of the destination MAC address are bitwise anded with EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMask. EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMarker is also bitwise anded with EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMask. If both results are the same, the Ethernet frame will be used as stream if the two least significant bytes of the destination MAC address match EthSwtPortPerStreamPolicerIdentifier.

######## 4. EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMask

- **UUID**: `edc76116-9485-4507-8fbe-ab3536b8f4ec`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMask`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `FF:FF:FF:FF`
  - 最大长度: 11
  - 最小长度: 11
- **描述**: See description of EthSwtPerStreamPolicingMarker

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtDerivative

- **UUID**: `b2d65778-5115-4b1e-9537-3382cfe4784d`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtDerivative`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]

  - `SJA1105R` (来源: Vector Informatik)

- **描述**: This parameter defines which derivative of the NXP switch the driver has to configure and control.

####### 1. EthSwtDemEventParameterRefs

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3ffda87f-6df6-46f4-8eaa-e2acd988f301`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtDemEventParameterRefs`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container for the references to DemEventParameter elements which shall be invoked using the API Dem_ReportErrorStatus in case the corresponding error occurs. The EventId is taken from the referenced DemEventParameter's DemEventId value. The standardized errors are provided in the container and can be extended by vendor specific error references.

- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_PORT_X_MII`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

- **需要索引**: false

  - `ETHSWT_PORT_BROAD_R_REACH` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_PORT_RTPGE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_PORT_X_MII` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of the interface on the pin level of the switch.

All ports of SJA1105PQRS have a xMII interface. Therefore ETHSWT_PORT_X_MII ist the only possible value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_PORT_X_MII:
xMII Interface

########## 4. EthSwtXMiiPhyType

- **UUID**: `93c19a4c-8a5d-495f-bf20-0f5964380dfa`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtXMiiPhyType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `PHY_MANAGED_BY_MCU`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PHY_MANAGED_BY_MCU` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `PHY_MANAGED_BY_SWITCH` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `NO_PHY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines if and how a PHY is connected to the switch xMII port.


PHY_MANAGED_BY_MCU:
The xMii interface of the port is connected to a PHY having its managment interface (MDIO/MDC) connected to a MCU.

PHY_MANAGED_BY_SWITCH:
The xMii interface of the port is connected to a PHY having its managment interface (MDIO/MDC) connected to the switch too.

NO_PHY:
The xMii interface of the port is directly connected to annother xMii interface without a PHY inbetween.

########## 5. EthSwtXMiiType

- **UUID**: `635bf84d-6f78-46a7-9116-3b470dd4c0f6`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtXMiiType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_MII`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_MII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_RMII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_RGMII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_SGMII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of MII-Interface used to connect to the ports counter part (MAC/PHY).

ETHSWT_MII:
Common MII interface allowing 10/100 MBit/s data connections.

ETHSWT_RMII:
Reduced MII interface allowing 10/100 MBit/s data connections.

ETHSWT_RGMII:
Reduced Gigabit MII Interface allowing 10/100/1000 MBit/s

ETHSWT_SGMII (SJA1105R/S Port 4 only):
Serial Gigabit MII Interface allowing 10/100/1000 MBit/s

########## 6. EthSwtOverridePortMode

- **UUID**: `c2247cac-feaa-4ce4-9aa5-457aae852565`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtOverridePortMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_PORT_MAC_MODE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_PORT_MAC_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_PORT_PHY_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter overrides the settings of EthSwtXMiiPhyType. This is only necessary for some hardware layouts, eg. when the host controller is connected via RMII and does not provide a REFCLK.
In most cases the configuration of the EthSwtXMiiPhyType parameter is sufficient.

########## 7. EthSwtPortSlewRate

- **UUID**: `1c94e30b-358a-4de0-991e-35035e678ff8`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSlewRate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_MEDIUM_SPEED_MODE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_HIGH_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_FAST_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_MEDIUM_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_LOW_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the slew rate for the port. Please refer to the chapter "Slew Rate Settings" of the NXP Sja1105PQRS Application Note. Based on the interface type, voltage and frequency recommended settings are provided for improving EMC performance. Using a wrong setting here can prevent correct communication on this port.

############# INTEGER 类型参数

  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_WEIGHTED_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

SJA1105PQRS only allows strict priority algorithm. Therefore ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY is the only value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY:
Strict priority algorithm.

########### 5. EthSwtPortVlanForwarding

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

############## 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

############# ENUMERATION 类型参数

############## 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

########### 6. EthSwtQbvScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bed2d511-8623-41e6-b8c8-4d1cff086e73`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtQbvScheduler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the Qbv scheduling

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration

- **UUID**: `ef2ef3b7-aa27-4f61-9ccf-3767ad360169`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1000000`
- **取值范围**: [1..52428800]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the duration the respective gate (FIFO) is opened and allowed to transmit frames.

Note: This duration must implcitly include the guard duration for the next entry.

############## 2. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset

- **UUID**: `a809fb1d-5dad-464a-8b74-7ad8760cfeca`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

############ 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

############# REFERENCE 类型引用

############## 1. EthSwtQbvSchedulerEntryFifoRef

- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..8]

- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/EthSwt_Sja1105PQRS/EthSwt/EthSwtConfig/EthSwtPort/EthSwtPortEgress/EthSwtPortFifo`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines which gate (FIFO) are allowed to send during the active time of this entry.

######### 2. EthSwtPortFifo

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `f562e231-595e-41a1-82c2-c937296a8cc6`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortFifo`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Represents a Fifo in the egress port.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortFifoTrafficClassAssignment

- **UUID**: `543c1bfe-14f0-48ed-adac-f23a5a193429`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortFifoTrafficClassAssignment`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..8]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the PCP assigned to the FIFO.

Note:
SJA1105PQRS hardware doesn't define an internal priority representation but works with the PCP of the VLAN-Tag. Therefore this assignment is no internal priority to FIFO assignment but a assignment of the VLAN-tags PCP.

############ 2. EthSwtPortFifoIdx

- **UUID**: `c5b5cfa5-5d79-47bb-bea7-370c95c970af`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortFifoIdx`

- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the index of this FIFO.

######### 6. EthSwtPortVlanForwarding

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

############ 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

######### 7. EthSwtQbvScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bed2d511-8623-41e6-b8c8-4d1cff086e73`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtQbvScheduler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the Qbv scheduling

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration

- **UUID**: `ef2ef3b7-aa27-4f61-9ccf-3767ad360169`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1000000`
- **取值范围**: [1..52428800]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the duration the respective gate (FIFO) is opened and allowed to transmit frames.

Note: This duration must implcitly include the guard duration for the next entry.

############ 2. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset

- **UUID**: `a809fb1d-5dad-464a-8b74-7ad8760cfeca`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

########## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines which gate (FIFO) are allowed to send during the active time of this entry.

########### 3. EthSwtPortPolicer

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortPolicer`
- **多重性**: [0..32760]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines rate policing parameters which are applied to ingress traffic.

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount

- **UUID**: `329ed8ee-21f7-44a7-8352-2ed8f734f04b`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `12500000`
- **取值范围**: [1..1250000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the amount of byte counts (excluding header information) which can be received in a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval.

############## 2. EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority

- **UUID**: `500056f3-84ab-428c-90e9-48c8a8559306`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the priority which this rate policy shall be limited on. If no priority is given this rate policy is not considering priority.

############## 3. EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax

- **UUID**: `9fc82372-6d21-4bda-9bb8-f06e7a316d34`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `15220`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum burst size in bytes for received frames. Its value is used to initialize the bandwidth budget on start-up. This parameter also defines the maximum bandwidth budget when no traffic associated with this entry has been received for a long time.

############# FLOAT 类型参数

############## 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval

- **UUID**: `666e9e1d-29c4-4167-805e-0ce78b6871ff`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..86400]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the time interval in seconds where a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount can be received without a rate limitation.

############# ENUMERATION 类型参数

############## 1. EthSwtPortRateViolationAction

- **UUID**: `df781b05-a88f-430b-8009-8bf42fcd98e2`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DROP_FRAME` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `BLOCK_SOURCE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the action to be taken when the rate policy criteria defined for this EthSwtPortPolicer are met.

- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

############ 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a3f57468-d208-4718-8f94-442ecce53644`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [0..31]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a2d0df71-a69f-41bc-baeb-99a6842733d7`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

############ 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay

- **UUID**: `f10e9ff1-c1d9-4510-b043-cd166c3bf222`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc

- **UUID**: `2fba7adb-a909-45bc-b371-84e5e0d1469c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the internal per port clock source, that is connected with the internal RGMII logic
and thus drives the transmitting side clock pins.
Internally, if the speed configured for this port is 1000 MBit/s, the RGMII logic is connected to PLL0,
which can provide the default phase output 0° as well as the two alternative phase outputs 120° and 240°.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0:
Select PLL0 with 0° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120:
Select PLL0 with 120° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240:
Select PLL0 with 240° phase output.

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortScheduler`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Represents a Scheduler in the egress port.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm

- **UUID**: `76bf0aa6-8f32-407c-9d9d-0516c6847314`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_DEFICIT_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_WEIGHTED_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

SJA1105PQRS only allows strict priority algorithm. Therefore ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY is the only value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY:
Strict priority algorithm.

######### 5. EthSwtPortVlanForwarding

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

############ 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

- **描述**: This parameter defines the PCP assigned to the FIFO.

**容器属性**:

- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortScheduler`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Represents a Scheduler in the egress port.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm

- **UUID**: `76bf0aa6-8f32-407c-9d9d-0516c6847314`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_DEFICIT_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_WEIGHTED_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

SJA1105PQRS only allows strict priority algorithm. Therefore ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY is the only value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY:
Strict priority algorithm.

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

########## 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

######### 3. EthSwtPortPolicer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3c57a27c-ff01-430a-b071-ef4d611700fd`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortPolicer`
- **多重性**: [0..32760]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines rate policing parameters which are applied to ingress traffic.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount

- **UUID**: `329ed8ee-21f7-44a7-8352-2ed8f734f04b`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `12500000`
- **取值范围**: [1..1250000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the amount of byte counts (excluding header information) which can be received in a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval.

############ 2. EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority

- **UUID**: `500056f3-84ab-428c-90e9-48c8a8559306`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the priority which this rate policy shall be limited on. If no priority is given this rate policy is not considering priority.

############ 3. EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax

- **UUID**: `9fc82372-6d21-4bda-9bb8-f06e7a316d34`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `15220`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum burst size in bytes for received frames. Its value is used to initialize the bandwidth budget on start-up. This parameter also defines the maximum bandwidth budget when no traffic associated with this entry has been received for a long time.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval

- **UUID**: `666e9e1d-29c4-4167-805e-0ce78b6871ff`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..86400]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the time interval in seconds where a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount can be received without a rate limitation.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRateViolationAction

- **UUID**: `df781b05-a88f-430b-8009-8bf42fcd98e2`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRateViolationAction`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DROP_FRAME`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DROP_FRAME` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `BLOCK_SOURCE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the action to be taken when the rate policy criteria defined for this EthSwtPortPolicer are met.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter provides the reference to the partition of the respective port policer.

####### 15. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `31e236f9-3bfa-4e62-afb2-5423844eaceb`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container allows configuration of RGMII specific parameters.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigRxDelay

- **UUID**: `41617315-34d1-4cc9-938b-d66bdf6ebea3`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigRxDelay`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [0..31]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

########## 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a3f57468-d208-4718-8f94-442ecce53644`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [0..31]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a2d0df71-a69f-41bc-baeb-99a6842733d7`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

########## 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay

- **UUID**: `f10e9ff1-c1d9-4510-b043-cd166c3bf222`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc

- **UUID**: `2fba7adb-a909-45bc-b371-84e5e0d1469c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the internal per port clock source, that is connected with the internal RGMII logic
and thus drives the transmitting side clock pins.
Internally, if the speed configured for this port is 1000 MBit/s, the RGMII logic is connected to PLL0,
which can provide the default phase output 0° as well as the two alternative phase outputs 120° and 240°.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0:
Select PLL0 with 0° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120:
Select PLL0 with 120° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240:
Select PLL0 with 240° phase output.

####### 23. EthSwtMemoryPartition

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7aee0b20-9569-4519-9a78-134034367dc6`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtMemoryPartition`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container defines the memory partitioning of the port policers and stream policers.
Note: The total number of assigned blocks (8 partitions for port policer memory PLUS 8 partitions for per stream policer memory) must not exceed 910 blocks if retagging is used or 929 blocks if retagging is not used.
(1 block = 128 bytes)

######## 4. EthSwtXMiiPhyType

- **UUID**: `93c19a4c-8a5d-495f-bf20-0f5964380dfa`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtXMiiPhyType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `PHY_MANAGED_BY_MCU`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PHY_MANAGED_BY_MCU` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `PHY_MANAGED_BY_SWITCH` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `NO_PHY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines if and how a PHY is connected to the switch xMII port.


PHY_MANAGED_BY_MCU:
The xMii interface of the port is connected to a PHY having its managment interface (MDIO/MDC) connected to a MCU.

PHY_MANAGED_BY_SWITCH:
The xMii interface of the port is connected to a PHY having its managment interface (MDIO/MDC) connected to the switch too.

NO_PHY:
The xMii interface of the port is directly connected to annother xMii interface without a PHY inbetween.

######## 5. EthSwtXMiiType

- **UUID**: `635bf84d-6f78-46a7-9116-3b470dd4c0f6`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtXMiiType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_MII`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_MII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_RMII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_RGMII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_SGMII` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of MII-Interface used to connect to the ports counter part (MAC/PHY).

ETHSWT_MII:
Common MII interface allowing 10/100 MBit/s data connections.

ETHSWT_RMII:
Reduced MII interface allowing 10/100 MBit/s data connections.

ETHSWT_RGMII:
Reduced Gigabit MII Interface allowing 10/100/1000 MBit/s

ETHSWT_SGMII (SJA1105R/S Port 4 only):
Serial Gigabit MII Interface allowing 10/100/1000 MBit/s

######## 6. EthSwtOverridePortMode

- **UUID**: `c2247cac-feaa-4ce4-9aa5-457aae852565`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtOverridePortMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_PORT_MAC_MODE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_PORT_MAC_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_PORT_PHY_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter overrides the settings of EthSwtXMiiPhyType. This is only necessary for some hardware layouts, eg. when the host controller is connected via RMII and does not provide a REFCLK.
In most cases the configuration of the EthSwtXMiiPhyType parameter is sufficient.

######## 7. EthSwtPortSlewRate

- **UUID**: `1c94e30b-358a-4de0-991e-35035e678ff8`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSlewRate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_HIGH_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_FAST_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_MEDIUM_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_LOW_SPEED_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the slew rate for the port. Please refer to the chapter "Slew Rate Settings" of the NXP Sja1105PQRS Application Note. Based on the interface type, voltage and frequency recommended settings are provided for improving EMC performance. Using a wrong setting here can prevent correct communication on this port.

############ 1. EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm

- **UUID**: `76bf0aa6-8f32-407c-9d9d-0516c6847314`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

######### 5. EthSwtPortVlanForwarding

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

############ 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

######### 6. EthSwtQbvScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bed2d511-8623-41e6-b8c8-4d1cff086e73`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtQbvScheduler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the Qbv scheduling

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration

- **UUID**: `ef2ef3b7-aa27-4f61-9ccf-3767ad360169`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1000000`
- **取值范围**: [1..52428800]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the duration the respective gate (FIFO) is opened and allowed to transmit frames.

Note: This duration must implcitly include the guard duration for the next entry.

############ 2. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset

- **UUID**: `a809fb1d-5dad-464a-8b74-7ad8760cfeca`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

########## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

########### REFERENCE 类型引用

############ 1. EthSwtQbvSchedulerEntryFifoRef

- **UUID**: `34972a0c-4df2-44e8-b649-c3e514f5654a`

- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..8]
- **目标**: `EthSwtPortFifo`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/EthSwt_Sja1105PQRS/EthSwt/EthSwtConfig/EthSwtPort/EthSwtPortEgress/EthSwtPortFifo`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines which gate (FIFO) are allowed to send during the active time of this entry.

####### 2. EthSwtPortFifo

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `f562e231-595e-41a1-82c2-c937296a8cc6`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortFifo`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Represents a Fifo in the egress port.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortFifoTrafficClassAssignment

- **UUID**: `543c1bfe-14f0-48ed-adac-f23a5a193429`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortFifoTrafficClassAssignment`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..8]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the PCP assigned to the FIFO.

Note:
SJA1105PQRS hardware doesn't define an internal priority representation but works with the PCP of the VLAN-Tag. Therefore this assignment is no internal priority to FIFO assignment but a assignment of the VLAN-tags PCP.

########## 2. EthSwtPortFifoIdx

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the index of this FIFO.

####### 3. EthSwtPortScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `35127f69-4c08-479a-a7e3-b10d58e82d2c`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortScheduler`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Represents a Scheduler in the egress port.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm

- **UUID**: `76bf0aa6-8f32-407c-9d9d-0516c6847314`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_DEFICIT_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_WEIGHTED_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

SJA1105PQRS only allows strict priority algorithm. Therefore ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY is the only value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY:
Strict priority algorithm.

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

####### 7. EthSwtQbvScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bed2d511-8623-41e6-b8c8-4d1cff086e73`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtQbvScheduler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the Qbv scheduling

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration

- **UUID**: `ef2ef3b7-aa27-4f61-9ccf-3767ad360169`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1000000`
- **取值范围**: [1..52428800]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the duration the respective gate (FIFO) is opened and allowed to transmit frames.

Note: This duration must implcitly include the guard duration for the next entry.

########## 2. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset

- **UUID**: `a809fb1d-5dad-464a-8b74-7ad8760cfeca`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. EthSwtQbvSchedulerEntryFifoRef

- **UUID**: `34972a0c-4df2-44e8-b649-c3e514f5654a`

- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..8]
- **目标**: `EthSwtPortFifo`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/EthSwt_Sja1105PQRS/EthSwt/EthSwtConfig/EthSwtPort/EthSwtPortEgress/EthSwtPortFifo`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines which gate (FIFO) are allowed to send during the active time of this entry.

TRUE:

######### 3. EthSwtPortPolicer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3c57a27c-ff01-430a-b071-ef4d611700fd`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortPolicer`
- **多重性**: [0..32760]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines rate policing parameters which are applied to ingress traffic.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount

- **UUID**: `329ed8ee-21f7-44a7-8352-2ed8f734f04b`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `12500000`
- **取值范围**: [1..1250000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the amount of byte counts (excluding header information) which can be received in a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval.

############ 2. EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority

- **UUID**: `500056f3-84ab-428c-90e9-48c8a8559306`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the priority which this rate policy shall be limited on. If no priority is given this rate policy is not considering priority.

############ 3. EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax

- **UUID**: `9fc82372-6d21-4bda-9bb8-f06e7a316d34`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `15220`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum burst size in bytes for received frames. Its value is used to initialize the bandwidth budget on start-up. This parameter also defines the maximum bandwidth budget when no traffic associated with this entry has been received for a long time.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval

- **UUID**: `666e9e1d-29c4-4167-805e-0ce78b6871ff`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..86400]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the time interval in seconds where a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount can be received without a rate limitation.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. EthSwtPortRateViolationAction

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DROP_FRAME` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `BLOCK_SOURCE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the action to be taken when the rate policy criteria defined for this EthSwtPortPolicer are met.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a2d0df71-a69f-41bc-baeb-99a6842733d7`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

########## 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay

- **UUID**: `f10e9ff1-c1d9-4510-b043-cd166c3bf222`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc

- **UUID**: `2fba7adb-a909-45bc-b371-84e5e0d1469c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the internal per port clock source, that is connected with the internal RGMII logic
and thus drives the transmitting side clock pins.
Internally, if the speed configured for this port is 1000 MBit/s, the RGMII logic is connected to PLL0,
which can provide the default phase output 0° as well as the two alternative phase outputs 120° and 240°.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0:
Select PLL0 with 0° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120:
Select PLL0 with 120° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240:
Select PLL0 with 240° phase output.

Represents a Scheduler in the egress port.

######## 参数配置

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm

- **UUID**: `76bf0aa6-8f32-407c-9d9d-0516c6847314`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_DEFICIT_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_WEIGHTED_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

SJA1105PQRS only allows strict priority algorithm. Therefore ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY is the only value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY:
Strict priority algorithm.

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

######## 参数配置

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

########## 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

####### 6. EthSwtQbvScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtQbvScheduler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the Qbv scheduling

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration

- **UUID**: `ef2ef3b7-aa27-4f61-9ccf-3767ad360169`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1000000`
- **取值范围**: [1..52428800]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the duration the respective gate (FIFO) is opened and allowed to transmit frames.

Note: This duration must implcitly include the guard duration for the next entry.

########## 2. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset

- **UUID**: `a809fb1d-5dad-464a-8b74-7ad8760cfeca`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

##### 7. EthSwtPortScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `35127f69-4c08-479a-a7e3-b10d58e82d2c`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortScheduler`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Represents a Scheduler in the egress port.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm

- **UUID**: `76bf0aa6-8f32-407c-9d9d-0516c6847314`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortSchedulerAlgorithm`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_DEFICIT_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_WEIGHTED_ROUND_ROBIN` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the scheduling algorithm used to schedule the predecessor elements configured by EthSwtPortSchedulerPredecessor.

SJA1105PQRS only allows strict priority algorithm. Therefore ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY is the only value allowed to be configured.

ETHSWT_SCHEDULER_STRICT_PRIORITY:
Strict priority algorithm.

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc042ee2-333b-4eb0-8c78-f73dc4152340`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwarding`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines how messages with a specific VLAN Id shall be handled at this egress port wrt. their VLAN Id.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority

- **UUID**: `39be9f17-1f51-4ce8-abb5-871aadbaa265`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanDefaultPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the default PCP applied to a VLAN tagged frame.
(not supported)

######## 2. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId

- **UUID**: `db8ca19b-1827-4707-a8c7-63e3222ec11e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4094]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the VLAN-ID the forwarding rule shall be applied to.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType

- **UUID**: `5fbf3dbb-f537-4c91-a4a2-98e5c7008c73`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortVlanForwardingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_NOT_SENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the rule that shall be applied to the VLAN tagged frame.

ETHSWT_NOT_SENT:
Do not forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN ID on this port.

ETHSWT_SENT_TAGGED:
Forward a frame with the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without changing the VLAN tag.

ETHSWT_SENT_UNTAGGED:
Forward a frame wiht the corresponding VLAN-ID on this port without the VLAN tag (tag will be removed).

##### 11. EthSwtQbvScheduler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bed2d511-8623-41e6-b8c8-4d1cff086e73`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtQbvScheduler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the Qbv scheduling

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration

- **UUID**: `ef2ef3b7-aa27-4f61-9ccf-3767ad360169`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryGateDuration`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1000000`
- **取值范围**: [1..52428800]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the duration the respective gate (FIFO) is opened and allowed to transmit frames.

Note: This duration must implcitly include the guard duration for the next entry.

######## 2. EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset

- **UUID**: `a809fb1d-5dad-464a-8b74-7ad8760cfeca`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtQbvScheduleEntryAbsoluteStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..10000000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the absolute time when the entry shall be scheduled.

If there are two or more entries for different ports at the same absolute time, they have to use the same FIFO.

The last entry has to switch off all FIFO.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false

####### 3. EthSwtPortPolicer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3c57a27c-ff01-430a-b071-ef4d611700fd`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortPolicer`
- **多重性**: [0..32760]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines rate policing parameters which are applied to ingress traffic.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount

- **UUID**: `329ed8ee-21f7-44a7-8352-2ed8f734f04b`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `12500000`
- **取值范围**: [1..1250000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the amount of byte counts (excluding header information) which can be received in a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval.

########## 2. EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority

- **UUID**: `500056f3-84ab-428c-90e9-48c8a8559306`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the priority which this rate policy shall be limited on. If no priority is given this rate policy is not considering priority.

########## 3. EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax

- **UUID**: `9fc82372-6d21-4bda-9bb8-f06e7a316d34`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `15220`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum burst size in bytes for received frames. Its value is used to initialize the bandwidth budget on start-up. This parameter also defines the maximum bandwidth budget when no traffic associated with this entry has been received for a long time.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval

##### 15. EthSwtPortPolicer

**容器描述**:

Defines rate policing parameters which are applied to ingress traffic.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortRatePolicedByteCount

- **UUID**: `329ed8ee-21f7-44a7-8352-2ed8f734f04b`

- **取值范围**: [1..1250000000]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the amount of byte counts (excluding header information) which can be received in a configured EthSwtPortRatePolicedTimeInterval.

######## 2. EthSwtPortRatePolicedPriority

- **UUID**: `500056f3-84ab-428c-90e9-48c8a8559306`

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the priority which this rate policy shall be limited on. If no priority is given this rate policy is not considering priority.

######## 3. EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax

- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRatePolicedSmax`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum burst size in bytes for received frames. Its value is used to initialize the bandwidth budget on start-up. This parameter also defines the maximum bandwidth budget when no traffic associated with this entry has been received for a long time.

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `31e236f9-3bfa-4e62-afb2-5423844eaceb`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container allows configuration of RGMII specific parameters.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigRxDelay

- **UUID**: `41617315-34d1-4cc9-938b-d66bdf6ebea3`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigRxDelay`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [0..31]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

######## 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a3f57468-d208-4718-8f94-442ecce53644`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [0..31]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter describes the delay between clock and data lines of the RGMII interface. Please refer to the NXP SJA1105PQRS manual for configuration details. This parameter is the "delay_value".

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay

- **UUID**: `a2d0df71-a69f-41bc-baeb-99a6842733d7`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableTxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

######## 2. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay

- **UUID**: `f10e9ff1-c1d9-4510-b043-cd166c3bf222`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigEnableRxDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the RxDelay for RGMII interfaces.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc

- **UUID**: `2fba7adb-a909-45bc-b371-84e5e0d1469c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPortRgmiiConfigTxClkSrc`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the internal per port clock source, that is connected with the internal RGMII logic
and thus drives the transmitting side clock pins.
Internally, if the speed configured for this port is 1000 MBit/s, the RGMII logic is connected to PLL0,
which can provide the default phase output 0° as well as the two alternative phase outputs 120° and 240°.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_0:
Select PLL0 with 0° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_120:
Select PLL0 with 120° phase output.

RGMII_TX_CLK_CLKSRC_PLL0_240:
Select PLL0 with 240° phase output.

##### 19. EthSwtPerStreamPolicer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `27177c95-4c26-4700-9fbd-9f04bf110beb`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicer`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container defines the different policing streams and how they are forwarded.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **4** 个配置容器：

####### 1. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStream

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `59fdd687-8fe6-4d19-98f2-4ac839fc3f9d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStream`
- **多重性**: [0..1024]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container defines a stream.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **6** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamIdentifier

- **UUID**: `7d8a76eb-b03c-475f-bc4b-e204c69a9ca1`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Identifier of the stream policer. The lower 16 bit of the Ethernet destination address must match the identifier.

########## 2. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMaximumFrameLength

- **UUID**: `b5c7a05b-c3d9-4b75-9f52-397dfeb61aac`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMaximumFrameLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2043`
- **取值范围**: [64..2043]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Maximum length of a frame which is accepted by the stream. This includes Ethernet destination and source address, Ethernet type and the frame checksum.

########## 3. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGap

- **UUID**: `76018cf2-ac2f-4dcb-9554-94717db363a8`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGap`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..1023]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Bandwith allocation gap as multiple of 100µs. If it is set to 0, the bag check is disabled.

########## 4. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGapJitter

- **UUID**: `a8a283fb-d3be-4fa1-a2f1-87a6bf7f4123`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGapJitter`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..1023]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Bandwidth allocation gap jitter in multiples of 10µs.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamPolicingType

- **UUID**: `378c0b4d-bf12-4f0f-907e-d20f314475fc`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamPolicingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_TIME_TRIGGERED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_TIME_TRIGGERED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_RATE_CONSTRAINT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the given stream is time triggered or rate constraint.

If it is time triggered, the following parameters are used to define the stream:
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamIdentifier
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamSourcePortRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMaximumFrameLength
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryType
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryDestinationPortRefs
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryEthSwtPerStreamPolicerFifoRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerScheduleEntryDirection
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerScheduleEntryDeltaTime
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerScheduleEntryForwardEntryRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerSubScheduleEntryIndex
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerSubScheduleEntryDeltaTime
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerSubScheduleEntryScheduleEntryRef

If it is rate constraint, the following parameters are used to define the stream:
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamIdentifier
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamSourcePortRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMaximumFrameLength
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGap
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGapJitter
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryType
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryDestinationPortRefs
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryEthSwtPerStreamPolicerFifoRef

Idependent of the policing type, mirroring of a stream can be define with these parameters:
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirror
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirrorDestinationPortRefs

########## 2. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirror

- **UUID**: `4465cc25-44ff-4cfa-82ee-0f8a00e75084`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirror`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_MIRROR_BOTH`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
