- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGap`

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamPolicingType

- **UUID**: `378c0b4d-bf12-4f0f-907e-d20f314475fc`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamPolicingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_TIME_TRIGGERED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_TIME_TRIGGERED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_RATE_CONSTRAINT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the given stream is time triggered or rate constraint.

If it is time triggered, the following parameters are used to define the stream:
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamIdentifier
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamSourcePortRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMaximumFrameLength
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryType
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryDestinationPortRefs
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryEthSwtPerStreamPolicerFifoRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerScheduleEntryDirection
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerScheduleEntryDeltaTime
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerScheduleEntryForwardEntryRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerSubScheduleEntryIndex
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerSubScheduleEntryDeltaTime
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerSubScheduleEntryScheduleEntryRef

If it is rate constraint, the following parameters are used to define the stream:
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamIdentifier
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamSourcePortRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMaximumFrameLength
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryRef
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGap
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamRateConstraintBandwidthAllocationGapJitter
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryType
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryDestinationPortRefs
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerForwardEntryEthSwtPerStreamPolicerFifoRef

Idependent of the policing type, mirroring of a stream can be define with these parameters:
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirror
EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirrorDestinationPortRefs

######## 2. EthSwtPerStreamPolicerStreamMirror

- **UUID**: `4465cc25-44ff-4cfa-82ee-0f8a00e75084`

  - `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_MIRROR_EGRESS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_MIRROR_BOTH` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines which frames shall be mirrored:
ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_MIRROR_INGRESS or
ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_MIRROR_EGRESS or
ETHSWT_STREAM_POLICING_MIRROR_BOTH.

##### 24. EthSwtMemoryPartition

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7aee0b20-9569-4519-9a78-134034367dc6`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthSwtMemoryPartition`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container defines the memory partitioning of the port policers and stream policers.
Note: The total number of assigned blocks (8 partitions for port policer memory PLUS 8 partitions for per stream policer memory) must not exceed 910 blocks if retagging is used or 929 blocks if retagging is not used.
(1 block = 128 bytes)

- **需要索引**: false

######## 2. EthSwtSpiHwTimeout

- **UUID**: `135d2fba-ff7b-4b51-bc8d-12ec29966cc3`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtSpiHwTimeout`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `300`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the amount of tries for SPI access that are executed until abort.

######## 3. EthSwtResetTimeout

- **UUID**: `665cfb32-7f27-4c5f-a348-ce900f0d316c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtResetTimeout`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `50`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum amount of tries the driver executes to check if the device has left the reset during intialization.

######## 4. EthSwtPllLockTimeout

- **UUID**: `f507952b-e5db-432c-b8c2-8c29c28a797c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtPllLockTimeout`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `50`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum amount of tries the driver executes to check if the PLL1 needed for RMII and RGMII clock generation has locked after configuration.

######## 5. EthSwtMaxArlTableEntries

- **UUID**: `a127ddc6-7229-40bc-b0ef-07aa2157a04d`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtMaxArlTableEntries`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..1024]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum amount of entries that can be stored to NvM during EthSwt_StoreConfiguration().

######## 6. EthSwtMaxParallelTsEvents

- **UUID**: `76be8d75-3c10-4298-867f-40fbeefe990e`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtMaxParallelTsEvents`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `10`
- **取值范围**: [2..100]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum amount of timestamping events the driver is able to process in parallel.

The value is related to the amount of ports that have timestamping enabled and the period/occurence of PTP frames that are timestamped.

######## 7. EthSwtMaxParallelMCastPortAssignments

- **UUID**: `e4623391-4f64-4c7a-b5b6-0bb808bb7c5a`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtMaxParallelMCastPortAssignments`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `5`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the amount of different Multicast-Addresses that can be handled by the EthSwt_UpdateMCastPortAssignment() API.

######## 8. EthSwtMaxParallelEvents

- **UUID**: `625f4e39-386e-4d97-977c-b1fb77052826`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtMaxParallelEvents`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `10`
- **取值范围**: [2..100]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum amount of management events the driver is able to process in parallel.

The value is related to the period/occurence of frames having a destination MAC address defined as to be managed (parameter 'Managed Frame Destination MAC address').

######## 10. EthSwtPersistentConfigurationResult

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthSwtApiCallType

- **UUID**: `307b5bbf-9f8c-4774-a3d7-375d3a12291c`
- **参数名**: `EthSwtApiCallType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETHSWT_SYNC_CALL`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETHSWT_SYNC_CALL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETHSWT_ASYNC_CALL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the calling mechanism used for selected AUTOSAR APIs.

The following APIs must be called in a syncronous way according to AUTOSAR:
- EthSwt_GetArlTable
- EthSwt_GetDropCount
- EthSwt_GetEtherStats

However the APIs consume a high amount of processing time proably harming the timing restrictions for task activation of the OS.
This extension allows to change the behavior of the API to just trigger the processing and get notificated if it has finished.

ETHSWT_SYNC_CALL:
Synchronous AUTOSAR behavior of API

ETHSWT_ASYNC_CALL:
Asynchronous behavior of API with finish callback
